"],en:["<h2>Signature Authentication</h2>","<h3>Generate an API Key</h3>",'To access private endpoints, you must create an API Key on the BingX website under User Center

If leaked, delete it immediately and create a new one.","<h3>Permission Settings</h3>","Newly created API Keys have read-only permission by default.","To place orders or perform other write operations, please enable the corresponding permissions in the UI.","<h3>Request Requirements</h3>","All authenticated REST requests must include:","X-BX-APIKEY in the request header.","signature as a request parameter, calculated using the signature algorithm.","timestamp (milliseconds) as the request time. The window can be adjusted using recvWindow.","<h3>Signature Description</h3>","The signature is generated using HMAC-SHA256 and returned as a 64-character lowercase hexadecimal string.","The signing and request construction rules are as follows:","1. Collect all business parameters (excluding signature).","2. Generate timestamp (milliseconds) and include it as a normal parameter.","3. Sort all parameters (business parameters + timestamp) by key in ASCII ascending order.","4. Build the signing string:","   key=value&key2=value2&...&amp;timestamp=xxx","   Parameter values must NOT be URL-encoded here.","5. Use secretKey to calculate HMAC-SHA256 over the signing string to obtain the signature.","<h3>URL Encoding Rules (Query String Only)</h3>","These rules apply only to query string parameters:","The signature is always calculated from the unencoded signing string.","If the signing string contains '[' or '{', URL-encode only parameter values in the actual request URL.","Do not encode parameter keys.","Use UTF-8 encoding and encode spaces as %20.","If the signing string does not contain '[' or '{', values do not need URL encoding.","<h3>Query String Example</h3>","Example parameters:","symbol=BTC-USDT","recvWindow=0","timestamp=1696751141337","Sorted signing string:","recvWindow=0&symbol=BTC-USDT&timestamp=1696751141337","Generate signature:","echo -n 'recvWindow=0&symbol=BTC-USDT&timestamp=1696751141337' | openssl dgst -sha256 -hmac 'SECRET_KEY' -hex","Send request:","https://open-api.bingx.com/xxx?recvWindow=0&symbol=BTC-USDT&timestamp=1696751141337&signature=...","<h3>Batch Order Query String Example</h3>","Example request URL:","https://open-api.bingx.com/openApi/spot/v1/trade/batchOrders?data=%5B%7B%22symbol%22%3A%22BTC-USDT%22%2C%22side%22%3A%22BUY%22%2C%22type%22%3A%22LIMIT%22%2C%22quantity%22%3A0.001%2C%22price%22%3A85000%2C%22newClientOrderId%22%3A%22%22%7D%2C%7B%22symbol%22%3A%22BTC-USDT%22%2C%22side%22%3A%22BUY%22%2C%22type%22%3A%22LIMIT%22%2C%22quantity%22%3A0.001%2C%22price%22%3A42000%2C%22newClientOrderId%22%3A%22%22%7D%5D&recvWindow=60000&timestamp=1766679008165&signature=87bd22cfb380dddf8ce6d2901be7f107fef23dcf1e3d066e1519d1daa8fa81c6","<h3>Request Body Example</h3>","The following example applies to endpoints that require application/json and read parameters from the request body.","Signature parameters (timestamp / signature) must be included in the request body.
